     1	"""Storage abstraction and in-memory implementation for knowledge discovery.
     2	
     3	Follows design.md §3:
     4	- Store interface defining contracts for agents, profiles, and messages.
     5	- InMemoryStore providing a standalone, testable implementation without Firestore dependency.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
    11	from copy import deepcopy
    12	import threading
    13	from typing import Any
    14	
    15	from knowledge_discovery.models import (
    16	    Agent,
    17	    Card,
    18	    MailSeed,
    19	    Message,
    20	    Profile,
    21	    Schedule,
    22	    Task,
    23	    utc_now_iso,
    24	)
    25	
    26	
    27	class Store(ABC):
    28	    """Abstract storage interface for knowledge discovery entities."""
    29	
    30	    # Agent operations (agents collection / registry)
    31	    @abstractmethod
    32	    def save_agent(self, agent: Agent) -> None:
    33	        """Save or update an agent record in the registry."""
    34	        pass
    35	
    36	    @abstractmethod
    37	    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    38	        """Retrieve an agent by agent_id."""
    39	        pass
    40	
    41	    @abstractmethod
    42	    def get_agent_by_employee_id(self, employee_id: str) -> Agent | None:
    43	        """Retrieve an agent by employee_id."""
    44	        pass
    45	
    46	    @abstractmethod
    47	    def list_agents(self, active_only: bool = False) -> list[Agent]:
    48	        """List registered agents, optionally filtering for active only."""
    49	        pass
    50	
    51	    # Profile operations (profiles collection)
    52	    @abstractmethod
    53	    def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> None:
    54	        """Save or update an employee knowledge profile."""
    55	        pass
    56	
    57	    @abstractmethod
    58	    def get_profile(self, employee_id: str) -> Profile | None:
    59	        """Retrieve a profile by employee_id."""
    60	        pass
    61	
    62	    @abstractmethod
    63	    def list_profiles(self) -> list[Profile]:
    64	        """List all employee profiles."""
    65	        pass
    66	
    67	    # Message operations (messages collection / audit log)
    68	    @abstractmethod
    69	    def save_message(self, message: Message) -> None:
    70	        """Save or update a message record."""
    71	        pass
    72	
    73	    @abstractmethod
    74	    def get_message(self, audit_id: str) -> Message | None:
    75	        """Retrieve a message by audit_id."""
    76	        pass
    77	
    78	    @abstractmethod
    79	    def list_messages(self, limit: int | None = None) -> list[Message]:
    80	        """List all messages in chronological order."""
    81	        pass
    82	
    83	    @abstractmethod
    84	    def get_messages_for_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> list[Message]:
    85	        """Retrieve messages involving the given entity (as sender, recipient, or participant)."""
    86	        pass
    87	
    88	    # Task operations (tasks collection §14.2)
    89	    @abstractmethod
    90	    def save_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
    91	        """Save or update a task record."""
    92	        pass
    93	
    94	    @abstractmethod
    95	    def list_tasks(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Task]:
    96	        """List tasks, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
    97	        pass
    98	
    99	    # Schedule operations (schedules collection §14.2)
   100	    @abstractmethod
   101	    def save_schedule(self, schedule: Schedule) -> None:
   102	        """Save or update a schedule reminder."""
   103	        pass
   104	
   105	    @abstractmethod
   106	    def list_schedules(self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None) -> list[Schedule]:
   107	        """List schedules, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id."""
   108	        pass
   109	
   110	    # MailSeed operations (mail_seeds collection §14.2, §14.5)
   111	    @abstractmethod
   112	    def save_mail_seed(self, mail: MailSeed) -> None:
   113	        """Save or update an email seed record."""
   114	        pass
   115	
   116	    @abstractmethod
   117	    def get_mail_seed(self, mail_id: str) -> MailSeed | None:
   118	        """Retrieve an email seed by mail_id."""
   119	        pass
   120	
   121	    @abstractmethod
   122	    def list_mail_seeds(
   123	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, unprocessed_only: bool = False
   124	    ) -> list[MailSeed]:
   125	        """List mail seeds, optionally filtered by owner and/or unprocessed status."""
   126	        pass
   127	
   128	    # Card operations (cards collection §14.2)
   129	    @abstractmethod
   130	    def save_card(self, card: Card) -> None:
   131	        """Save or update a secretary card."""
   132	        pass
   133	
   134	    @abstractmethod
   135	    def get_card(self, card_id: str) -> Card | None:
   136	        """Retrieve a card by card_id."""
   137	        pass
   138	
   139	    @abstractmethod
   140	    def list_cards(
   141	        self, owner_employee_id: str | None = None, status: str | None = None
   142	    ) -> list[Card]:
   143	        """List cards, optionally filtered by owner_employee_id and/or status."""
   144	        pass
   145	
   146	    @abstractmethod
   147	    def find_open_card_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> Card | None:
   148	        """Find an open stagnation card for the given task and owner."""
   149	        pass
   150	
   151	    @abstractmethod
   152	    def find_cards_for_task(self, owner_employee_id: str, task_id: str) -> list[Card]:
   153	        """Find all stagnation cards for the given task and owner."""
   154	        pass
   155	
   156	    @abstractmethod
   157	    def try_confirm_card(self, card_id: str) -> tuple[Card | None, bool]:
   158	        """Atomically transition a card's status from 'open' to 'confirmed' (CAS, §14.4).
   159	
   160	        Returns (card, won):
   161	        - (None, False) if no card with this card_id exists.
   162	        - (card, True) if THIS call performed the open->confirmed transition; the
   163	          returned card reflects the post-transition ('confirmed') state.
   164	        - (card, False) if the card exists but was not in 'open' status (already
   165	          'confirmed' by a concurrent caller, or 'dismissed'/'resolved'/'applied');
   166	          the returned card reflects its current stored state.
   167	
   168	        Implementations must guarantee at most one caller ever observes won=True
   169	        for a given card_id (Firestore: @firestore.transactional; in-memory: a
   170	        lock guarding the read-check-write).
   171	        """
   172	        pass
   173	
   174	    @abstractmethod
   175	    def clear(self) -> None:
   176	        """Clear all stored data (useful for test isolation)."""
   177	        pass
   178	
   179	
   180	class InMemoryStore(Store):
   181	    """In-memory implementation of Store using Python data structures."""
   182	
   183	    def __init__(self) -> None:
   184	        self._agents: dict[str, Agent] = {}
   185	        self._profiles: dict[str, Profile] = {}
   186	        self._messages: list[Message] = []
   187	        self._messages_by_id: dict[str, Message] = {}
   188	        self._tasks: dict[str, Task] = {}
   189	        self._schedules: dict[str, Schedule] = {}
   190	        self._mail_seeds: dict[str, MailSeed] = {}
   250	        from_entity = data.get("from") or data.get("from_entity", "")
   251	        to_entity = data.get("to") or data.get("to_entity", "")
   252	        return cls(
   253	            audit_id=data["audit_id"],
   254	            from_entity=from_entity,
   255	            to_entity=to_entity,
   256	            intent=data.get("intent", ""),
   257	            payload_type=data.get("payload_type", ""),
   258	            payload=dict(data.get("payload", {})),
   259	            audit_payload=dict(data["audit_payload"]) if data.get("audit_payload") is not None else None,
   260	            consent_state=data.get("consent_state", "n/a"),
   261	            timestamp=data.get("timestamp", utc_now_iso()),
   262	            rejected=data.get("rejected", False),
   263	        )
   264	
   265	
   266	@dataclass
   267	class ConnectionDetails:
   268	    """Details of a verbalized connection between query and profile."""
   269	
   270	    reason_text: str
   271	    score: float
   272	
   273	
   274	@dataclass
   275	class ConnectionInferenceResult:
   276	    """Result of Stage 2 independent connection inference for a single candidate.
   277	
   278	    Attributes:
   279	        connection: ConnectionDetails if meaningful connection found; None if no connection.
   280	        no_connection_reason: Explanation if connection is None.
   281	        cited_item_keys: Profile item keys cited during inference (must be present even when connection is None).
   282	    """
   283	
   284	    connection: ConnectionDetails | None
   285	    no_connection_reason: str | None = None
   286	    cited_item_keys: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   287	
   288	
   289	@dataclass
   290	class FunnelCandidate:
   291	    """Candidate representation for Screen Funnel (scale demonstration)."""
   292	
   293	    employee_id: str
   294	    name: str
   295	    role: str
   296	    similarity: float
   297	
   298	
   299	@dataclass
   300	class RequesterCandidateStatus:
   301	    """Candidate status as viewed from requester UI.
   302	
   303	    Never reveals whether connection was based on private profile items.
   304	    Status state values:
   305	    - 'pending': 返答待ち
   306	    - 'matched': つながりました（MTG提案あり）
   307	    - 'declined': 今回は難しいそうです（理由+添付表示）
   308	    """
   309	
   310	    candidate_id: str
   311	    candidate_name: str
   312	    state: str  # 'pending' | 'matched' | 'declined'
   313	    meeting_duration: int | None = None
   314	    decline_reason: str | None = None
   315	    attachment: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   316	
   317	
   318	@dataclass
   319	class Task:
   320	    """Task entity for secretary stagnation detection (§14.2).
   321	
   322	    Attributes:
   323	        task_id: Unique task identifier.
   324	        owner_employee_id: Identifier of the employee owning this task.
   325	        title: Task title.
   326	        description: Detailed task description.
   327	        status: 'todo' | 'in_progress' | 'done'.
   328	        due_date: Due date in ISO format (YYYY-MM-DD).
   329	        created_at: ISO8601 creation timestamp.
   330	        last_updated_at: ISO8601 last updated timestamp.
   331	        reschedule_count: Number of times this task has been rescheduled.
   332	        status_changed_at: ISO8601 timestamp of last status change.
   333	    """
   334	
   335	    task_id: str
   336	    owner_employee_id: str
   337	    title: str
   338	    description: str = ""
   339	    status: str = "todo"  # "todo" | "in_progress" | "done"
   340	    due_date: str = ""
   341	    created_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
   342	    last_updated_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
   343	    reschedule_count: int = 0
   344	    status_changed_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
   345	
   346	    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   347	        return {
   348	            "task_id": self.task_id,
   349	            "owner_employee_id": self.owner_employee_id,
   350	            "title": self.title,
   351	            "description": self.description,
   352	            "status": self.status,
   353	            "due_date": self.due_date,
   354	            "created_at": self.created_at,
   355	            "last_updated_at": self.last_updated_at,
   356	            "reschedule_count": self.reschedule_count,
   357	            "status_changed_at": self.status_changed_at,
   358	        }
   359	
   360	    @classmethod
   361	    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Task:
   362	        return cls(
   363	            task_id=data["task_id"],
   364	            owner_employee_id=data["owner_employee_id"],
   365	            title=data.get("title", ""),
   366	            description=data.get("description", ""),
   367	            status=data.get("status", "todo"),
   368	            due_date=data.get("due_date", ""),
   369	            created_at=data.get("created_at", utc_now_iso()),
   370	            last_updated_at=data.get("last_updated_at", utc_now_iso()),
   371	            reschedule_count=int(data.get("reschedule_count", 0)),
   372	            status_changed_at=data.get("status_changed_at", utc_now_iso()),
   373	        )
   374	
   375	
   376	@dataclass
   377	class Schedule:
   378	    """Schedule reminder entity for morning digest (§14.2).
   379	
   380	    Attributes:
   381	        item_id: Unique schedule identifier.
   382	        owner_employee_id: Identifier of the employee.
   383	        kind: 'expense_deadline' | 'weekly_report' | 'monthly_report' |
   384	              'meeting_prep' | 'meeting_review' | 'journal'.
   385	        title: Schedule title / description.
   386	        due_date: Due date in ISO format (YYYY-MM-DD).
   387	    """
   388	
   389	    item_id: str
   390	    owner_employee_id: str
   391	    kind: str
   392	    title: str
   393	    due_date: str
   394	
   395	    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   396	        return {
   397	            "item_id": self.item_id,
   398	            "owner_employee_id": self.owner_employee_id,
   399	            "kind": self.kind,
   400	            "title": self.title,
   401	            "due_date": self.due_date,
   402	        }
   403	
   404	    @classmethod
   405	    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Schedule:
   406	        return cls(
   407	            item_id=data["item_id"],
   408	            owner_employee_id=data["owner_employee_id"],
   409	            kind=data.get("kind", ""),
   410	            title=data.get("title", ""),
   411	            due_date=data.get("due_date", ""),
   412	        )
   413	
   414	
   415	@dataclass
   416	class MailSeed:
   417	    """Email seed record for proactive profile diff proposals (§14.2, §14.5).
   418	
   419	    Attributes:
   420	        mail_id: Unique mail identifier.
   421	        owner_employee_id: Employee owning the mailbox.
   422	        subject: Mail subject line.
   423	        body: Mail body text.
   424	        received_at: ISO8601 received timestamp.
   425	        processed: Whether a profile diff card has been generated.
   426	    """
   427	
   428	    mail_id: str
   429	    owner_employee_id: str
   430	    subject: str
   431	    body: str
   432	    received_at: str = field(default_factory=utc_now_iso)
   433	    processed: bool = False
   434	
   435	    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   436	        return {
   437	            "mail_id": self.mail_id,
   438	            "owner_employee_id": self.owner_employee_id,
   439	            "subject": self.subject,
   440	            "body": self.body,
   441	            "received_at": self.received_at,
   442	            "processed": self.processed,
   443	        }
   444	
   445	    @classmethod
   446	    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> MailSeed:
   447	        return cls(
   448	            mail_id=data["mail_id"],
   449	            owner_employee_id=data["owner_employee_id"],
   450	            subject=data.get("subject", ""),
   451	            body=data.get("body", ""),
   452	            received_at=data.get("received_at", utc_now_iso()),
   453	            processed=bool(data.get("processed", False)),
   454	        )
   455	
   456	
   457	@dataclass
   458	class Card:
   459	    """Secretary card for stagnation alerts or profile diff proposals (§14.2).
   460	
   461	    Attributes:
   462	        card_id: Unique card identifier.
   463	        owner_employee_id: Identifier of the card recipient employee.
   464	        type: 'stagnation' | 'profile_diff'.
   465	        tier: 'notice' | 'request_draft' | None.
   466	        payload: Card payload (stagnation details or profile diff proposal).
   467	        status: 'open' | 'confirmed' | 'dismissed' | 'applied' | 'resolved'.
   468	        resolved_reason: 'task_done' | 'score_below_t1' | None.
   469	        linked_query_audit_id: Linked query audit_id if confirmed.
   470	        created_at: ISO8601 timestamp.
   471	        updated_at: ISO8601 timestamp.
   472	    """
   473	
   474	    card_id: str
   475	    owner_employee_id: str
   476	    type: str  # "stagnation" | "profile_diff"
   477	    tier: str | None = None  # "notice" | "request_draft" | None
   478	    payload: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
   479	    status: str = "open"  # "open" | "confirmed" | "dismissed" | "applied" | "resolved"
   480	    resolved_reason: str | None = None  # "task_done" | "score_below_t1" | None

   297	              <div style="margin-top:10px;">
   298	                <label style="color:#cbd5e1; font-size:12px; font-weight:600;">🤖 AI Inquiry Draft (Review & Edit):</label>
   299	                <textarea id="draft_${esc(card.card_id)}" style="height:65px; margin-bottom:6px;">${esc(p.question_draft || "")}</textarea>
   300	                <div class="fixed-notice">Candidates may differ at request time — the full search runs only after you confirm.</div>
   301	                <div class="btn-group">
   302	                  <button onclick="confirmStagnationCard('${esc(card.card_id)}')">Request an intro</button>
   303	                  <button class="secondary" onclick="dismissCard('${esc(card.card_id)}')">Dismiss</button>
   304	                </div>
   305	              </div>
   306	            ` : `
   307	              <div style="margin-top:8px;">
   308	                <div style="font-size:12px; color:var(--text-muted); margin-bottom:8px;">This task is experiencing stagnation. Consider reviewing or reaching out to colleagues.</div>
   309	                <button class="secondary" onclick="dismissCard('${esc(card.card_id)}')">Dismiss</button>
   310	              </div>
   311	            `}
   312	          </div>`;
   313	      });
   314	
   315	      // Profile diff cards (§14.5)
   316	      diffCards.forEach(card => {
   317	        const p = card.payload || {};
   318	        cardsHtml += `
   319	          <div class="diff-box" id="card_box_${esc(card.card_id)}">
   320	            <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
   321	              <span style="font-size:13px; font-weight:600; color:#6ee7b7;">📄 Profile Update Suggestion</span>
   322	              <span class="badge" style="background:#064e3b; color:#a7f3d0;">From recent email</span>
   323	            </div>
   324	            <div style="font-size:12px; color:#cbd5e1; margin-top:6px;">
   325	              <strong>Subject:</strong> ${esc(p.subject || "Email")}
   326	            </div>
   327	            <div class="diff-preview">[${esc(p.item_key || "current_work")}]: ${esc(p.body_draft || "")}</div>
   328	            <div id="diff_edit_box_${esc(card.card_id)}" style="display:none; margin-bottom:10px;">
   329	              <textarea id="diff_edit_text_${esc(card.card_id)}" style="height:60px;">${esc(p.body_draft || "")}</textarea>
   330	              <button onclick="submitEditDiff('${esc(card.card_id)}')">Save & Apply</button>
   331	            </div>
   332	            <div class="btn-group" style="margin-top:6px;">
   333	              <button onclick="reviewDiff('${esc(card.card_id)}', 'apply')">Apply (Public)</button>
   334	              <button class="secondary" onclick="toggleEditDiff('${esc(card.card_id)}')">Edit & Apply</button>
   335	              <button class="secondary" onclick="reviewDiff('${esc(card.card_id)}', 'private_apply')">Apply as Private</button>
   336	              <button class="danger-btn" onclick="reviewDiff('${esc(card.card_id)}', 'dismiss')">Dismiss</button>
   337	            </div>
   338	          </div>`;
   339	      });
   340	
   341	      cardsContainer.innerHTML = cardsHtml;
   342	    }
   343	
   344	    async function confirmStagnationCard(cardId) {
   345	      const textarea = document.getElementById(`draft_${cardId}`);
   346	      const editedQuestion = textarea ? textarea.value.trim() : "";
   347	      if (!editedQuestion) {
   348	        alert("Please enter a question to dispatch.");
   349	        return;
   350	      }
   351	
   352	      try {
   353	        const res = await fetch("/api/secretary/confirm", {
   354	          method: "POST",
   355	          headers: {
   356	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   357	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   358	          },
   359	          body: JSON.stringify({
   360	            card_id: cardId,
   361	            edited_question: editedQuestion
   362	          })
   363	        });
   364	        const data = await res.json();
   365	        if (!res.ok) {
   366	          alert("Confirm failed: " + (data.detail || "Error"));
   367	          return;
   368	        }
   369	
   370	        document.getElementById("queryMeta").innerText = 
   371	          `Confirmed Inquiry ID: ${data.query_audit_id} | Dispatched via Secretary Confirm`;
   372	        document.getElementById("liveStatus").style.display = "inline-flex";
   373	
   374	        await fetchDigest();
   375	        await fetchStatuses();
   376	
   377	        if (pollInterval) clearInterval(pollInterval);
   378	        pollInterval = setInterval(fetchStatuses, 2000);
   379	      } catch (err) {
   380	        alert("Confirm error: " + err.message);
   381	      }
   382	    }
   383	
   384	    async function dismissCard(cardId) {
   385	      try {
   386	        const res = await fetch(`/api/secretary/cards/${encodeURIComponent(cardId)}/dismiss`, {
   387	          method: "POST",
   388	          headers: { "X-API-Key": API_KEY }
   389	        });
   390	        if (!res.ok) {
   391	          alert("Dismiss failed");
   392	          return;
   393	        }
   394	        await fetchDigest();
   395	      } catch (err) {
   396	        alert("Dismiss error: " + err.message);
   397	      }
   398	    }
   399	
   400	    function toggleEditDiff(cardId) {
   401	      const box = document.getElementById(`diff_edit_box_${cardId}`);
   402	      if (box) {
   403	        box.style.display = (box.style.display === "none") ? "block" : "none";
   404	      }
   405	    }

   407	    async function submitEditDiff(cardId) {
   408	      const textEl = document.getElementById(`diff_edit_text_${cardId}`);
   409	      const edited = textEl ? textEl.value.trim() : "";
   410	      await reviewDiff(cardId, "edit_apply", edited);
   411	    }
   412	
   413	    async function reviewDiff(cardId, action, editedBody = null) {
   414	      try {
   415	        const res = await fetch(`/api/secretary/profile-diff/${encodeURIComponent(cardId)}/review`, {
   416	          method: "POST",
   417	          headers: {
   418	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   419	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   420	          },
   421	          body: JSON.stringify({
   422	            action: action,
   423	            edited_body: editedBody
   424	          })
   425	        });
   426	        const data = await res.json();
   427	        if (!res.ok) {
   428	          alert("Review failed: " + (data.detail || "Error"));
   429	          return;
   430	        }
   431	        await fetchDigest();
   432	      } catch (err) {
   433	        alert("Review error: " + err.message);
   434	      }
   435	    }

   441	    async function submitInquiry() {
   442	      const q = document.getElementById("questionText").value.trim();
   443	      if (!q) {
   444	        alert("Please enter a question.");
   445	        return;
   446	      }
   447	      const submitBtn = document.getElementById("submitBtn");
   448	      submitBtn.disabled = true;
   449	      submitBtn.innerText = "Dispatching...";
   450	
   451	      try {
   452	        const res = await fetch("/api/query", {
   453	          method: "POST",
   454	          headers: {
   455	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   456	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   457	          },
   458	          body: JSON.stringify({
   459	            requester_id: REQUESTER_ID,
   460	            question_text: q
   461	          })
   462	        });
   463	        const data = await res.json();
   464	        if (!res.ok) {
   465	          alert("Error dispatching query: " + (data.detail || "Unknown error"));
   466	          return;
   467	        }
   468	
   469	        document.getElementById("queryMeta").innerText = 
   470	          `Inquiry ID: ${data.query_id} | Dispatched to ${data.dispatched_count} candidate agent(s) | Top 20 screened in Screen Funnel`;
   471	        
   472	        document.getElementById("liveStatus").style.display = "inline-flex";
   473	        await fetchStatuses();
   474	
   475	        if (pollInterval) clearInterval(pollInterval);
   476	        pollInterval = setInterval(fetchStatuses, 2000);
   477	      } catch (err) {
   478	        alert("Failed to connect to server: " + err.message);
   479	      } finally {
   480	        submitBtn.disabled = false;
   481	        submitBtn.innerText = "Dispatch Inquiry";
   482	      }
   483	    }
   484	
   485	    async function fetchStatuses() {
   486	      try {
   487	        const res = await fetch(`/api/requester/${REQUESTER_ID}/status`, {
   488	          headers: { "X-API-Key": API_KEY }
   489	        });
   490	        if (!res.ok) return;
   491	        const data = await res.json();
   492	        renderStatuses(data.statuses || []);
   493	      } catch (err) {
   494	        console.error("Status fetch error:", err);
   495	      }
   496	    }
   497	
   498	    function renderStatuses(statuses) {
   499	      const container = document.getElementById("statusContainer");
   500	      if (!statuses || statuses.length === 0) {
   501	        container.innerHTML = `
   502	          <div style="font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 24px;">
   503	            No candidate responses recorded yet.
   504	          </div>`;
   505	        return;
   506	      }
   507	
   508	      container.innerHTML = statuses.map((item, idx) => {
   509	        if (item.state === "pending") {
   510	          return `
   511	            <div class="status-card pending">
   512	              <div class="status-header">
   513	                <span class="status-title">Candidate #${idx + 1}</span>
   514	                <span class="status-tag pending">Waiting for response</span>
   515	              </div>
   516	              <div class="status-body">
   517	                Candidate's personal agent is evaluating tacit background fit. Awaiting candidate review and consent.
   518	              </div>
   519	            </div>`;
   520	        } else if (item.state === "matched") {
   521	          return `
   522	            <div class="status-card matched">
   523	              <div class="status-header">
   524	                <span class="status-title">Connected with ${esc(item.respondent_name) || "Candidate"}</span>
   525	                <span class="status-tag matched">Connected</span>
   526	              </div>
   527	              <div class="status-body">
   528	                <strong>Synergy Match Established:</strong> ${esc(item.respondent_name)} consented to connect!
   529	                <br>Proposed: <strong>15-minute introductory meeting</strong>.
   530	              </div>
   531	            </div>`;
   532	        } else if (item.state === "declined") {
   533	          let attHtml = "";
   534	          if (item.attachment) {
   535	            if (item.attachment.type === "link") {
   536	              attHtml = `<div class="attachment-box">📎 <strong>Shared Link:</strong> <a href="${/^https?:\/\//.test(item.attachment.content) ? esc(item.attachment.content) : "#"}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${esc(item.attachment.content)}</a></div>`;
   537	            } else if (item.attachment.type === "doc") {
   538	              attHtml = `<div class="attachment-box">📄 <strong>Shared Document:</strong> <a href="/attachments/${encodeURIComponent(item.attachment.content)}?api_key=${encodeURIComponent(API_KEY)}" target="_blank">${esc(item.attachment.content)}</a></div>`;
   539	            } else {
   540	              attHtml = `<div class="attachment-box">📝 <strong>Note:</strong> ${esc(item.attachment.content)}</div>`;
   541	            }
   542	          }
   543	          return `
   544	            <div class="status-card declined">
   545	              <div class="status-header">
   546	                <span class="status-title">${esc(item.respondent_name) || "Candidate"}</span>
   547	                <span class="status-tag declined">Unavailable this time</span>
   548	              </div>
   549	              <div class="status-body">
   550	                ${item.decline_reason ? `<em>"${esc(item.decline_reason)}"</em>` : "Candidate cannot connect at this time."}
    80	        <h2 style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: 600;">Incoming Inquiries & Synergy Asks</h2>
    81	        <button class="secondary" style="padding: 4px 10px; font-size: 12px;" onclick="loadAsksForAgent()">Refresh</button>
    82	      </div>
    83	
    84	      <div id="asksContainer">
    85	        <div style="font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 24px;">
    86	          Loading received asks...
    87	        </div>
    88	      </div>
    89	    </div>
    90	  </div>

    92	  <script>
    93	    const API_KEY = (new URLSearchParams(location.search)).get("api_key") || "";
    94	    if (!API_KEY) {
    95	      document.body.insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin",
    96	        '<div style="background:#b91c1c;color:#fff;padding:8px 16px;font-size:13px;">Missing api_key: open this page as /PAGE?api_key=YOUR_KEY</div>');
    97	    }
    98	    // Escape server-derived strings before HTML interpolation (S-4)
    99	    function esc(v) {
   100	      return String(v ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c]));
   101	    }
   102	
   103	    async function loadAsksForAgent() {
   104	      const agentId = document.getElementById("agentSelector").value;
   105	      const container = document.getElementById("asksContainer");
   106	      container.innerHTML = `<div style="font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 24px;">Fetching asks for ${esc(agentId)}...</div>`;
   107	
   108	      try {
   109	        const res = await fetch(`/api/candidate/${agentId}/asks`, {
   110	          headers: { "X-API-Key": API_KEY }
   111	        });
   112	        if (!res.ok) {
   113	          container.innerHTML = `<div style="color: var(--danger); font-size: 13px;">Error fetching asks.</div>`;
   114	          return;
   115	        }
   116	        const data = await res.json();
   117	        renderAsks(data.asks || []);
   118	      } catch (err) {
   119	        container.innerHTML = `<div style="color: var(--danger); font-size: 13px;">Connection error: ${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
   120	      }
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    function renderAsks(asks) {
   124	      const container = document.getElementById("asksContainer");
   125	      if (!asks || asks.length === 0) {
   126	        container.innerHTML = `
   127	          <div style="font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 24px;">
   128	            No incoming synergy requests for this agent.
   129	          </div>`;
   130	        return;
   131	      }
   132	
   133	      container.innerHTML = asks.map((ask) => {
   134	        const isPrivate = ask.is_private || ask.intent === "connect_ask_private";
   135	        const privateBadge = isPrivate
   136	          ? `<span class="private-badge">🔒 Related to your private profile item</span>`
   137	          : "";
   138	
   139	        const cardClass = isPrivate ? "ask-card private-border" : "ask-card";
   140	        const isPending = (ask.consent_state === "pending" || ask.consent_state === "n/a");
   141	
   142	        let actionArea = "";
   143	        if (isPending) {
   144	          actionArea = `
   145	            <div style="display: flex; gap: 10px; margin-top: 14px;">
   146	              <button class="success" onclick="giveConsent('${ask.ask_audit_id}', 'granted')">Accept & Connect (15-min MTG)</button>
   147	              <button class="secondary" onclick="toggleDeclineForm('${ask.ask_audit_id}')">Decline with Note / Attachment</button>
   148	            </div>
   149	            <div id="declineForm_${ask.ask_audit_id}" class="decline-panel" style="display: none;">
   150	              <label>Decline Reason / Note:</label>
   151	              <textarea id="declineReason_${ask.ask_audit_id}" placeholder="e.g. Currently unavailable due to client escalations, but sharing relevant resources below..."></textarea>
   152	              
   153	              <div class="form-row">
   154	                <select id="attType_${ask.ask_audit_id}" onchange="updateAttachmentPlaceholder('${ask.ask_audit_id}')">
   155	                  <option value="link">Link (URL)</option>
   156	                  <option value="doc">Document ID</option>
   157	                  <option value="text">Text Note</option>
   158	                </select>
   159	                <input type="text" id="attContent_${ask.ask_audit_id}" placeholder="https://wiki.internal/clinic-relocation" value="https://wiki.internal/clinic-relocation">
   160	              </div>
   161	
   162	              <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px;">
   163	                <button class="danger-outline" onclick="giveConsent('${ask.ask_audit_id}', 'declined')">Confirm Decline & Send Resources</button>
   164	                <button class="secondary" onclick="toggleDeclineForm('${ask.ask_audit_id}')">Cancel</button>
   165	              </div>
   166	            </div>`;
   167	        } else {
   168	          actionArea = `
   169	            <div style="margin-top: 12px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-muted);">
   170	              Status: <strong>${ask.consent_state.toUpperCase()}</strong>
   171	            </div>`;
   172	        }
   173	
   174	        return `
   175	          <div class="${cardClass}">
   176	            <div class="ask-header">
   177	              <span class="ask-title">Synergy Inquiry from Colleague</span>
   178	              <div>${privateBadge}</div>
   179	            </div>
   180	            <div style="font-size: 14px; color: #f1f5f9; margin-bottom: 8px;">
   181	              <strong>Inquiry:</strong> "${esc(ask.question_summary) || "Colleague inquiry"}"
   182	            </div>
   183	            <div class="reason-box">
   184	              <strong>🤖 AI Matching Rationale:</strong><br>
   185	              ${esc(ask.reason_text) || "Relevant tacit experience detected."}
   186	              <div style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--text-muted); margin-top: 6px;">
   187	                Confidence Score: ${Math.round((ask.score || 0.8) * 100)}%
   188	              </div>
   189	            </div>
   190	            ${actionArea}
   191	          </div>`;
   192	      }).join("");
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    function toggleDeclineForm(auditId) {
   196	      const form = document.getElementById(`declineForm_${auditId}`);
   197	      form.style.display = form.style.display === "none" ? "block" : "none";
   198	    }
   199	
   200	    function updateAttachmentPlaceholder(auditId) {
   201	      const type = document.getElementById(`attType_${auditId}`).value;
   202	      const input = document.getElementById(`attContent_${auditId}`);
   203	      if (type === "link") {
   204	        input.value = "https://wiki.internal/clinic-relocation";
   205	      } else if (type === "doc") {
   206	        input.value = "doc_clinic_relocation_guide";
   207	      } else {
   208	        input.value = "Please refer to standard outpatient guidelines.";
   209	      }
   210	    }
   211	
   212	    async function giveConsent(auditId, decision) {
   213	      const agentId = document.getElementById("agentSelector").value;
   214	      let reasonText = "";
   215	      let attachment = null;
   216	
   217	      if (decision === "declined") {
   218	        reasonText = document.getElementById(`declineReason_${auditId}`)?.value.trim() || "Currently unable to participate.";
   219	        const attType = document.getElementById(`attType_${auditId}`)?.value;
   220	        const attContent = document.getElementById(`attContent_${auditId}`)?.value.trim();
   221	        if (attContent) {
   222	          attachment = { type: attType, content: attContent };
   223	        }
   224	      }
   225	
   226	      try {
   227	        const res = await fetch(`/api/candidate/${agentId}/consent`, {
   228	          method: "POST",
   229	          headers: {
   230	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   231	            "X-API-Key": API_KEY,
   232	          },
   233	          body: JSON.stringify({
   234	            ask_audit_id: auditId,
   235	            decision: decision,
   236	            reason_text: reasonText,
   237	            attachment: attachment
   238	          })
   239	        });
   240	
   241	        if (!res.ok) {
   242	          const errData = await res.json();
   243	          alert("Failed to submit consent decision: " + (errData.detail || "Unknown error"));
   244	          return;
   245	        }
   246	
   247	        await loadAsksForAgent();
   248	      } catch (err) {
   249	        alert("Connection error: " + err.message);
   250	      }
   251	    }
   252	
   253	    // Initial load
   254	    window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", loadAsksForAgent);
   255	  </script>
   256	</body>
   257	</html>
   620	def populate_store(
   621	    store: Store,
   622	    embedder: Embedder | None = None,
   623	    dry_run: bool = False,
   624	    today: str | None = None,
   625	) -> tuple[int, int]:
   626	    """Populate store with seed agents, profiles, and Milestone 3 secretary records.
   627	
   628	    Returns:
   629	        (agent_count, profile_count)
   630	    """
   631	    agents, profiles = generate_all_seeds(embedder=embedder)
   632	    m3_tasks = build_m3_tasks(today_str=today)
   633	    m3_schedules = build_m3_schedules(today_str=today)
   634	    m3_mail_seeds = build_m3_mail_seeds(today_str=today)
   635	
   636	    if dry_run:
   637	        print(f"=== DRY RUN: Seed Data Summary ===")
   638	        print(f"Total Agents to register:  {len(agents)}")
   639	        print(f"Total Profiles to store:    {len(profiles)}")
   640	        print(f"Total M3 Tasks to store:    {len(m3_tasks)}")
   641	        print(f"Total M3 Schedules to store:{len(m3_schedules)}")
   642	        print(f"Total M3 MailSeeds to store:{len(m3_mail_seeds)}")
   643	        print("\n--- Registered Agents (4) ---")
   644	        for a in agents:
   645	            print(f"  • {a.agent_id}: {a.display_name} (employee_id={a.employee_id}, active={a.active})")
   646	        print("\n--- Sample Profiles (First 5) ---")
   647	        for p in profiles[:5]:
   648	            priv_keys = [it.key for it in p.items if it.visibility == "private"]
   649	            priv_info = f" [Private items: {priv_keys}]" if priv_keys else ""
   650	            print(f"  • {p.employee_id}: {p.name} - {p.role}{priv_info}")
   651	            for it in p.items:
   652	                print(f"      [{it.key}] ({it.visibility}): {it.body[:65]}...")
   653	        return len(agents), len(profiles)
   654	
   655	    # Save agents to store
   656	    for a in agents:
   657	        store.save_agent(a)
   658	
   659	    # Save profiles to store
   660	    for p in profiles:
   661	        store.save_profile(p)
   662	
   663	    # Save M3 secretary seeds
   664	    for t in m3_tasks:
   665	        store.save_task(t)
   666	
   667	    for s in m3_schedules:
   668	        store.save_schedule(s)
   669	
   670	    for m in m3_mail_seeds:
   671	        store.save_mail_seed(m)
   672	
   673	    print(
   674	        f"Successfully populated store with {len(agents)} agents, {len(profiles)} profiles, "
   675	        f"{len(m3_tasks)} tasks, {len(m3_schedules)} schedules, and {len(m3_mail_seeds)} mail seeds."
   676	    )
   677	    return len(agents), len(profiles)
   678	
   679	
   680	def main() -> None:
   681	    """CLI entrypoint for seed generation script."""
   682	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Generate and load seed data for knowledge-discovery.")
   683	    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Print summary without writing to Firestore.")
   684	    parser.add_argument("--use-firestore", action="store_true", help="Write to live Firestore instance.")
   685	    parser.add_argument("--project", type=str, default=None, help="Google Cloud project ID.")
   686	    parser.add_argument("--database", type=str, default=None, help="Firestore database ID.")
   687	    parser.add_argument(
   688	        "--today",
   689	        type=str,
   690	        default=None,
   691	        help="Base date for relative seed generation (YYYY-MM-DD). Defaults to DEMO_TODAY or today.",
   692	    )
   693	    parser.add_argument(
   694	        "--clear",
   695	        action="store_true",
   696	        help="Delete all collections (agents/profiles/messages/tasks/schedules/mail_seeds/cards) "
   697	        "before seeding. Use for a demo reset (requires --use-firestore).",
   698	    )
   699	    parser.add_argument(
   700	        "--embedder",
   701	        choices=["deterministic", "gemini"],
   702	        default="deterministic",
   703	        help="Embedder used for stored profile embeddings. Use 'gemini' when the server "
   704	        "runs with GeminiEmbedder (embedding dimensions must match at query time).",
   705	    )
   706	    args = parser.parse_args()
   707	
   708	    embedder = None
   709	    if args.embedder == "gemini":
   710	        from knowledge_discovery.gemini_adapters import GeminiEmbedder
   711	        embedder = GeminiEmbedder()
   712	
   713	    if args.dry_run or not args.use_firestore:
   714	        from knowledge_discovery.store import InMemoryStore
   715	        store = InMemoryStore()
   716	        populate_store(store, dry_run=args.dry_run, embedder=embedder, today=args.today)
   717	    else:
   718	        from knowledge_discovery.firestore_store import FirestoreStore
   719	        store = FirestoreStore(project=args.project, database=args.database)
   720	        if args.clear:
   721	            print("Clearing all collections before seeding (--clear)...")
   722	            store.clear()
   723	        populate_store(store, dry_run=False, embedder=embedder, today=args.today)
   724	
   725	
   726	if __name__ == "__main__":
   727	    main()

   317	    def run_sweep(self, demo_today: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
   318	        """Execute full secretary sweep across all tasks and mail seeds (§14.1, §14.2, §14.5).
   319	
   320	        State Machine Rules (§14.2):
   321	        1. Confirmed or Dismissed tasks: Do NOT recreate cards.
   322	        2. Task status 'done' or score < T1: Transition open card to 'resolved'.
   323	        3. T1 <= score < T2: Create or update 'notice' card.
   324	        4. score >= T2: Run preview search (public only, embedding_public). If candidates found,

   469	                                }

   472	                        }

   475	                        card_id = f"card_stag_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}"

   520	                        was_notice = (open_card.tier == "notice")
   521	                        open_card.tier = tier
   522	                        open_card.payload["score"] = score
   523	                        open_card.payload["evidence_line"] = evidence_line
   524	                        open_card.payload["task_title"] = task.title
   525	                        open_card.payload["question_draft"] = q_draft
   526	                        open_card.payload["preview"] = preview_dict
   527	                        open_card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()

   530	                        if was_notice and tier == "request_draft":
   531	                            cards_promoted += 1
   532	                        else:

   590	                )

   592	            # Mark processed
   593	            mail.processed = True
   594	            self.store.save_mail_seed(mail)

   598	            "date": ref_today.isoformat(),
   599	            "tasks_evaluated": tasks_evaluated,
   600	            "cards_created": cards_created,
   601	            "cards_promoted": cards_promoted,

   603	            "cards_resolved": cards_resolved,
   604	            "diff_cards_created": diff_cards_created,

   607	    def get_morning_digest(
   608	        self,
   609	        employee_id: str,
   610	        demo_today: str | None = None,
   610	        demo_today: str | None = None,
   611	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   612	        """Assemble dynamic morning digest for employee (§14.2, §14.8).
   613	
   614	        Schedule rule order: Overdue -> Today -> Tomorrow -> Upcoming.
   615	        Followed by open stagnation and profile_diff cards.
   616	        """
   617	        ref_today = get_today(demo_today)
   618	        all_schedules = self.store.list_schedules(owner_employee_id=employee_id)
   619	
   620	        # Categorize and order schedule reminders
   621	        reminders: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   622	        for s in all_schedules:
   623	            due_d = _parse_date_or_timestamp(s.due_date)
   624	            if due_d is None:
   625	                category = "upcoming"
   626	                priority = 4
   627	            elif due_d < ref_today:
   628	                category = "overdue"
   629	                priority = 1
   630	            elif due_d == ref_today:
   631	                category = "today"
   632	                priority = 2
   633	            elif (due_d - ref_today).days == 1:
   634	                category = "tomorrow"
   635	                priority = 3
   636	            else:
   637	                category = "upcoming"
   638	                priority = 4
   639	
   640	            reminders.append({
   641	                "item_id": s.item_id,
   642	                "kind": s.kind,
   643	                "title": s.title,
   644	                "due_date": s.due_date,
   645	                "due_category": category,
   646	                "_priority": priority,
   647	            })
   648	
   649	        # Sort: priority ascending, then due_date ascending
   650	        reminders.sort(key=lambda r: (r["_priority"], r["due_date"]))
   651	        for r in reminders:
   652	            r.pop("_priority", None)
   653	
   654	        # Retrieve open cards
   655	        open_cards = self.store.list_cards(owner_employee_id=employee_id, status="open")
   656	        stagnation_cards = [
   657	            c.to_dict() for c in open_cards if c.type == "stagnation"
   658	        ]
   659	        profile_diff_cards = [
   660	            c.to_dict() for c in open_cards if c.type == "profile_diff"
   661	        ]
   662	
   663	        return {
   664	            "employee_id": employee_id,
   665	            "date": ref_today.isoformat(),
   666	            "reminders": reminders,
   667	            "stagnation_cards": stagnation_cards,
   668	            "profile_diff_cards": profile_diff_cards,
   669	        }
   670	
   671	    def confirm_stagnation_card(
   672	        self,
   673	        card_id: str,
   674	        edited_question: str,
   675	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   675	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   676	        """Confirm stagnation card and dispatch query via standard query path (§14.4).
   677	
   678	        Atomic CAS (X-3, V-6/S-8):
   679	        - Store.try_confirm_card() atomically transitions open->confirmed; only the
   680	          caller that wins the race proceeds to submit the query.
   681	        - A losing concurrent call (card already 'confirmed') returns the existing
   682	          linked_query_audit_id without resubmitting.
   683	        - If not 'open' and not 'confirmed', raises an error.
   684	        - On query execution failure, rolls back card status to 'open'.
   685	        """
   686	        card, won = self.store.try_confirm_card(card_id)
   687	        if card is None:
   688	            raise ValueError(f"Secretary card '{card_id}' not found")
   689	
   690	        if not won:
   691	            if card.status == "confirmed":
   692	                return {
   693	                    "status": "already_confirmed",
   694	                    "card_id": card.card_id,
   695	                    "query_audit_id": card.linked_query_audit_id,
   696	                }
   697	            raise ValueError(f"Card '{card_id}' cannot be confirmed in status '{card.status}'")
   698	
   699	        try:
   700	            # Pass edited question to existing query pipeline
   701	            result = self.kd_service.submit_query(
   702	                requester_id=card.owner_employee_id,
   703	                question_text=edited_question,
   704	            )
   705	            query_audit_id = result.query_message.audit_id
   706	            card.linked_query_audit_id = query_audit_id
   707	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   708	            self.store.save_card(card)
   709	
   710	            return {
   711	                "status": "confirmed",
   712	                "card_id": card.card_id,
   713	                "query_audit_id": query_audit_id,
   714	                "dispatched_count": len(result.dispatched_asks),
   715	            }
   716	        except Exception as exc:
   717	            # Rollback on submission failure
   718	            card.status = "open"
   719	            card.linked_query_audit_id = None
   720	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   721	            self.store.save_card(card)
   722	            raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to submit discovery query: {exc}") from exc
   723	
   724	    def review_profile_diff(
   725	        self,
   726	        card_id: str,
   727	        action: str,  # 'apply' | 'edit_apply' | 'private_apply' | 'dismiss'
   728	        edited_body: str | None = None,
   729	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   730	        """Process employee review for a profile diff proposal card (§14.5).
   731	
   732	        Actions:
   733	        - 'apply': Add a NEW item with visibility='public', regenerate embeddings, card -> 'applied'.
   734	        - 'edit_apply': Add a NEW item with the edited body (public), regenerate embeddings, card -> 'applied'.
   735	        - 'private_apply': Add a NEW item with visibility='private', regenerate embeddings, card -> 'applied'.
   736	        - 'dismiss': Card -> 'dismissed' (no profile change).
   737	
   738	        Reflection is always additive (design §14.5-3, V-7/S-7): existing profile
   739	        items are never overwritten and their visibility is never flipped. If the
   740	        proposed item_key collides with an existing item, a unique suffixed key is
   741	        used instead. The item_key itself always comes from the card's own payload
   742	        (never from the caller) so a request cannot retarget which item gets edited.
   743	        """
   744	        card = self.store.get_card(card_id)
   745	        if card is None or card.type != "profile_diff":
   746	            raise ValueError(f"Profile diff card '{card_id}' not found")
   747	        if card.status != "open":
   748	            raise ValueError(f"Card '{card_id}' is already {card.status}")
   749	
   750	        if action == "dismiss":
   751	            card.status = "dismissed"
   752	            card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   753	            self.store.save_card(card)
   754	            return {"status": "dismissed", "card_id": card_id}
   755	
   756	        if action not in ("apply", "edit_apply", "private_apply"):
   757	            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported review action '{action}'")
   758	
   759	        key = card.payload.get("item_key", "current_work")
   760	        body = (
   761	            edited_body.strip()
   762	            if (action == "edit_apply" and edited_body and edited_body.strip())
   763	            else str(card.payload.get("body_draft", "")).strip()
   764	        )
   765	        visibility = "private" if action == "private_apply" else "public"
   766	
   767	        # Reflection assumes the profile already exists (V-10): the secretary
   768	        # never fabricates a profile for a mail owner who isn't a registered
   769	        # employee.
   770	        prof = self.store.get_profile(card.owner_employee_id)
   771	        if prof is None:
   772	            raise LookupError(
   773	                f"Profile for employee '{card.owner_employee_id}' not found; cannot apply diff"
   774	            )
   775	
   776	        # Additive only: never overwrite an existing key/visibility. On
   777	        # collision, append a uniquely suffixed key instead (design §14.5-3).
   778	        if prof.get_item(key) is not None:
   779	            suffix = 1
   780	            candidate_key = f"{key}_mail_{suffix}"
   781	            while prof.get_item(candidate_key) is not None:
   782	                suffix += 1
   783	                candidate_key = f"{key}_mail_{suffix}"
   784	            key = candidate_key
   785	
   786	        new_item = ProfileItem(
   787	            key=key,
   788	            body=body,
   789	            source="mail_seed",
   790	            visibility=visibility,
   791	            reviewed=True,
   792	        )
   793	        prof.items.append(new_item)
   794	
   795	        # Regenerate BOTH full embedding and public embedding (§14.5, §9.3)
   796	        self.matching_engine.compute_profile_embedding(prof)
   797	        self.store.save_profile(prof)
   798	
   799	        # Update card status
   800	        card.status = "applied"
   801	        card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   802	        self.store.save_card(card)
   803	
   804	        return {
   805	            "status": "applied",
   806	            "card_id": card_id,
   807	            "item_key": key,
   808	            "visibility": visibility,
   809	            "reviewed": True,
   810	        }
   811	
   812	    def dismiss_card(self, card_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   813	        """Dismiss an open stagnation card (§14.2, E-8).
   814	
   815	        Restricted to type='stagnation' and status='open': profile_diff cards
   816	        have their own dedicated 'see later' path via review_profile_diff(action='dismiss'),
   817	        and confirmed/applied/resolved cards must not be reachable through this
   818	        generic endpoint.
   819	        """
   820	        card = self.store.get_card(card_id)
   821	        if card is None or card.type != "stagnation":
   822	            raise ValueError(f"Stagnation card '{card_id}' not found")
   823	        if card.status != "open":
   824	            raise ValueError(f"Card '{card_id}' cannot be dismissed in status '{card.status}'")
   825	        card.status = "dismissed"
   826	        card.updated_at = utc_now_iso()
   827	        self.store.save_card(card)
   828	        return {"status": "dismissed", "card_id": card_id}

     1	"""Agent Engine app wrapper (design §14.7 入口1: 定期起動 = 決定的オペレーション).
     2	
     3	run_daily_sweep is registered as a standard (non-stream) Agent Engine
     4	operation -- invoked over the `:query` REST verb, since vertexai.agent_engines
     5	treats the "" key of register_operations() as the standard/non-stream API
     6	mode. It never touches the LLM: it is a thin passthrough to
     7	SecretaryApiClient.run_sweep() and its raw JSON result.
     8	
     9	Any non-2xx/unreachable Cloud Run response raises out of
    10	SecretaryApiClient.run_sweep() (see client.py) and is deliberately not
    11	caught here, so the Agent Engine `:query` response itself becomes non-2xx
    12	and Cloud Scheduler records the attempt as a failure and retries
    13	(design §14.7 C-31: failures are not silenced).
    14	"""
    15	
    16	from __future__ import annotations
    17	
    18	import asyncio
    19	from typing import Any
    20	
    21	from vertexai.agent_engines import AdkApp
    22	
    23	from .agent import build_secretary_llm_agent
    24	from .client import SecretaryApiClient
    25	
    26	
    27	class SecretaryApp(AdkApp):
    28	    """AdkApp subclass exposing run_daily_sweep as a standard operation."""
    29	
    30	    def __init__(self, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
    31	        kwargs.setdefault("agent", build_secretary_llm_agent())
    32	        super().__init__(**kwargs)
    33	
    34	    async def run_daily_sweep(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
    35	        """Deterministic entry point for Cloud Scheduler (§14.7 入口1).
    36	
    37	        No LLM involvement: calls the existing Cloud Run
    38	        POST /api/secretary/sweep and returns its JSON result unchanged.
    39	
    40	        Declared async and executed in a worker thread so the ~10s blocking
    41	        HTTP call never stalls the Agent Engine server's event loop: a
    42	        synchronous version made the replica unresponsive to health probes
    43	        mid-sweep and Vertex answered "Service Unavailable" (HTTP 400
    44	        FAILED_PRECONDITION) even though the sweep completed on Cloud Run.
    45	        """
    46	        client = SecretaryApiClient()
    47	        return await asyncio.to_thread(client.run_sweep)
    48	
    49	    def register_operations(self) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    50	        """Publishes run_daily_sweep as a standard *async* (non-stream)
    51	        operation -- still invoked via the `:query` verb -- in addition to
    52	        AdkApp's default session-management operations."""
    53	        operations = super().register_operations()
    54	        operations["async"] = [*operations.get("async", []), "run_daily_sweep"]
    55	        return operations
     1	"""Read-only dialogue agent (design §14.7 入口2: 対話 = LLMエージェント).
     2	
     3	Tools: get_my_digest only. employee_id is never a tool argument -- it is
     4	derived from the ADK session's user_id via ToolContext, so a session can
     5	only ever read its own owner's digest (C-34: no reading someone else's
     6	digest). No write-capable tool (confirm/dismiss/review) is exposed here,
     7	and run_daily_sweep is deliberately NOT registered as an LLM tool (C-33:
     8	a chat session must not resolve cards or consume mail_seeds as a side
     9	effect of conversation).
    10	"""
    11	
    12	from __future__ import annotations
    13	
    14	import os
    15	from functools import cached_property
    16	
    17	from google.adk.agents import LlmAgent
    18	from google.adk.models import Gemini
    19	from google.adk.tools import ToolContext
    20	from google.genai import Client
    21	
    22	from .client import SecretaryApiClient
    23	
    24	# Gemini 3.7 Flash per design §14.7.
    25	_MODEL_NAME = "gemini-3.7-flash"
    26	
    27	INSTRUCTION = (
    28	    "You are the employee's personal morning secretary. You have exactly "
    29	    "one tool, get_my_digest, which returns today's due-date reminders and "
    30	    "any open stagnation or profile-diff cards for the current user. "
    31	    "Never speculate about stagnation the tool result does not mention -- "
    32	    "only summarize what the tool actually returned. Always present your "
    33	    "reply as your own (the AI's) summary, never as something the user "
    34	    "said or wrote. Reply in English."
    35	)
    36	
    37	
    38	class _GlobalGemini(Gemini):
    39	    """Gemini pinned to the `global` model endpoint (design §14.7, Y-2対応).
    40	
    41	    Agent Engine injects GOOGLE_CLOUD_LOCATION for wherever the Runtime is
    42	    deployed (asia-northeast1), but the Gemini 3.7 Flash model endpoint is
    43	    only served from global/us/eu. Relying on an env var override would tie
    44	    correctness to what Runtime happens to inject, so instead this uses
    45	    ADK's documented extension point for pinning client options the Gemini
    46	    fields don't expose directly (see google.adk.models.google_llm.Gemini's
    47	    class docstring: subclass and override the `api_client` cached
    48	    property) to construct the genai Client with location="global" set
    49	    explicitly, independent of GOOGLE_CLOUD_LOCATION.
    50	    """
    51	
    52	    @cached_property
    53	    def api_client(self) -> Client:
    54	        return Client(
    55	            vertexai=True,
    56	            project=os.environ.get("GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT"),
    57	            location="global",
    58	        )
    59	
    60	
    61	def _build_client() -> SecretaryApiClient:
    62	    """Factory seam so tests can substitute a fake client without touching
    63	    real env vars or the network."""
    64	    return SecretaryApiClient()
    65	
    66	
    67	def get_my_digest(tool_context: ToolContext) -> dict:
    68	    """Fetch today's morning digest (reminders + open cards) for the current
    69	    session's user.
    70	
    71	    Takes no employee_id argument by design (§14.7 C-34): the owner is
    72	    always the session's own user_id, so this tool can never be used to
    73	    read someone else's digest.
    74	    """
    75	    return _build_client().fetch_digest(employee_id=tool_context.user_id)
    76	
    77	
    78	def build_secretary_llm_agent() -> LlmAgent:
    79	    """Builds the read-only dialogue agent (入口2)."""
    80	    return LlmAgent(
    81	        name="secretary",
    82	        model=_GlobalGemini(model=_MODEL_NAME),
    83	        instruction=INSTRUCTION,
    84	        tools=[get_my_digest],
    85	    )

   227	## 検討済み・却下（参考）
   228	
   229	- **2レーン構造（情報レーンで文書引用のみ返す＋接続レーン）— v2で却下**。理由: 事務的な単純事実確認はRAGの領域であり本プロダクトが解く問題ではない。文書引用のみで完結する経路を持つと既存RAG製品と差がなくなり陳腐化する。本プロダクトが扱う問い合わせは常に人的接続に価値があるという前提に立ち返った
   230	- **辞退の不可視化 — v2で却下**。理由: 「断ることを容易にする」というハブ保護の発想（旧決定5）を撤回。AIによる効率化が進む中でこそ人が時間を割いて向き合うべき、という価値観に転換。理由付きの丁寧な辞退は正常な社会的やり取りとして可視化する
   231	- エージェント間を全てenum/boolに縛る案 — 却下（マッチ精度が出ず過剰）
   232	- 被マッチ月間クォータ＋2番手ルーティング — 却下（同意ベースの保護で足りる）
   233	- クエリへのpurpose enum（情報取得/繋がりたい）付与 — 却下（v2では単一レーンのため、そもそも区別が不要になった）
   234	- 非同期テキスト中継レーン — 却下
   235	- 属人化リスクの経営向け検出 — スコープ外へ
   236	- 1on1支援との同時提出 — 却下（Zenn vol.5へ分離）
   237	- デモ舞台の医療機関固定 — 却下（作者は医療コンサル所属で現場当事者ではなく、ドメイン知識不足により不自然になるため）
   238	- プロフィールに給与等の個人情報を含める想定 — 却下（v2で明記。design.md批評で誤って持ち込まれた例。プロフィールは仕事内容・得意分野・職歴のみ）
   239	- **秘書層の外部OSS基盤（openclaw / openagent 等）の組み込み — 却下（2026-08-19）**。理由: 「タスクを見て停滞を検知しプロアクティブに動く」単機能のOSS部品は存在せず、いずれもスタンドアロンの大型アシスタント基盤。10日でCloud Run＋ADK構成へ統合するより薄い自前実装の方が確実に安い。規約上はライセンス遵守でOSS利用可だが、審査の得点は Gemini/ADK 自作部分にしか付かない
   240	- **Gemini Enterprise（SaaS）の Agent Designer スケジュール実行の利用 — 却下（2026-08-19）**。理由: per-seat ライセンスが別途必要でハッカソン文脈に合わず、ADK自作部分の評価も薄まる。秘書の定期起動は Cloud Scheduler＋自作ADKエージェントで実装する
   241	- **Gemini Spark の実接続をクリティカルパスに載せる — 却下（2026-08-19）**。理由: AI Ultra 契約・提供地域の制約に加え、個人アカウント（Gmail/カレンダー）連携はプラットフォーム自体のセキュリティ設計が必要で、社内前提の本プロダクトと境界が合わない。write-up・アーキ図では「先々の検討候補（接続可否・監査ログ等のセキュリティ面が未確認）」としてのみ触れ、本番構成とは呼ばない（2026-08-23 文言を下方修正）
   242	
   243	## v1からの主な変更点（サマリ）
   244	
   245	| 項目 | v1 | v2 | v3 | v4 |
   246	|---|---|---|---|---|
   247	| レーン構造 | 2レーン（情報レーン＋接続レーン） | 単一レーン（つながりレーン） | 同左 | 同左 |
   248	| 単純事実確認 | レーン1で対応 | 非スコープ（RAG等の別ツールの領域） | 同左 | 同左 |
   249	| AIの生成 | レーン1で引用選定・回答生成 | 質問はそのまま転送。AIは要約・生成しない | 同左 | **生成制限を撤廃。AI発言として明示すれば要約・推論・紹介文いずれも自由**（本人名義で流通させないの一点のみ残す） |
   250	| 辞退の可視性 | 依頼者に不可視 | 理由付きで依頼者に表示 | 同左 | 理由に加え**資料・リンク・一言回答を任意添付可能** |
   251	| プロフィール内容 | 未定義（悪性クエリ例で給与が誤って混入） | 仕事内容・得意分野・簡易な職歴に限定 | 現在の仕事内容を詳細化して追加（暗黙知マッチングの主要材料） | 同左 |
   252	| 非公開の対象 | 未定義 | NDA対象の業務情報のみ | 同左 | **本人が意図的に公開から外したもの全般。本人のエージェントだけが知っており、関連質問が来たら本人にのみ打診する**（マッチング除外の死んだデータから、情報主権の実体機能へ反転） |
   253	| マッチング精度 | 未定義（確定的な回答を前提としていた） | 未定義 | **確定情報ではなく探索的・確率的なマッチングを明示的に許容**（プロダクトの核心的差別化） | 同左 |
   254	| プロダクト定義 | 「誰に聞けばいいかを解決する」 | 同左 | **「人と人の暗黙知を繋いでシナジーを生み出す」を根本思想として明文化** | 同左 |
   447	## 16. 接続部品（spec v8 FR25〜30。v12追補）
   448	
   449	**背骨**: 3つとも「差し替え点（インターフェース）＋実装2つ（デモ用／本番用）＋envで切替」の同型。デモ経路（`demo_key`／1テナント／`seed`）の挙動は**変えない**（既存テスト97件＋B段13件が不変であることが回帰の定義）。既存の配送・同意・監査・マッチングのコードには触れない。
   450	
   451	### 16.1 認証の差し替え点（FR25〜26）
   452	
   453	```
   454	Principal { tenant_id: str, employee_id: str | None, mode: "demo_key" | "iap", email: str | None }
   455	PrincipalResolver.resolve(request) -> Principal   # 失敗は 401
   456	  ├─ DemoKeyResolver : 現行 verify_api_key と同一判定（X-API-Key / api_key）。tenant = 台帳の既定テナント、employee_id = None（各APIは従来どおり申告値を使う）
   457	  └─ IapResolver     : X-Goog-IAP-JWT-Assertion を google-auth で検証（ES256、IAP公開鍵 https://www.gstatic.com/iap/verify/public_key、audience = env IAP_AUDIENCE）
   458	                       → claim email → 台帳の email_domains でテナント確定 → テナントDBの identities/{email} で employee_id 確定。無ければ 403
   459	```
   460	
   461	- FastAPI の依存を `verify_api_key` から `get_principal` に置換する（全エンドポイント共通の1箇所）。`AUTH_MODE=demo_key|iap`（既定 demo_key）
   462	- **本人性突合（FR26）**: employee_id を受ける全API（`/api/secretary/*`、`/api/requester/{id}/status`、`/api/candidate/{agent_id}/*`）に `require_self(principal, employee_id)` を通す。`iap` モードで不一致なら 403、`demo_key` モードでは素通し（現行）。candidate 系は agent_id→employee_id を `agents` で引いて比較
   463	- **未検証ヘッダでの認証はしない**: `X-Goog-Authenticated-User-Email` は参考表示にのみ使い、認証判定は JWT 検証のみ。IAP を経由しないアクセス経路を残さないことは Cloud Run 側の設定（IAP統合＋`--no-allow-unauthenticated`）で担保し README に手順を書く（本体のデモサービスには掛けない＝鍵方式のUIと両立しないため。実機確認は任意の別サービスで。spec未決）
   464	- `/api/me` を追加（principal の mode/tenant/employee を返す）。requester.html / candidate.html は起動時に `/api/me` を見て、`iap` のときはペルソナ切替を隠し本人固定にする（demo_key では従来どおり）
   465	- `identities/{email} → employee_id` はテナントDBのコレクション。シードで4ペルソナ＋Jordan に `@meridian-care.example` の架空メールを投入
   466	- 依存: `google-auth`（google-cloud-firestore 経由で導入済み）のみ。新規パッケージなし
   467	
   468	### 16.2 テナント＝Firestoreデータベース分離（FR27）
   469	
   470	```
   471	TenantRegistry（env TENANTS_JSON、既定 [{"tenant_id":"meridian","database":"(default)","email_domains":["meridian-care.example"]}]）
   472	TenantContext { store, service(KnowledgeDiscoveryService), secretary(SecretaryService), matching }   # テナントごとに1組、遅延生成・キャッシュ
   473	ContextRouter.for_tenant(tenant_id) -> TenantContext
   474	```
   475	
   476	- 既存の「アプリ起動時に store/service/secretary を1組作る」構造を「テナントごとに1組」に一般化する。`demo_key` では既定テナント1組なので挙動は同一。InMemory でも同じルータ（テストは2テナントを InMemory で構成）
   477	- **到達不能性**: リクエストは `principal.tenant_id` の TenantContext だけを受け取る。他テナントの store を参照するコードパスは存在しない（横断クエリを書く場所がない）。これを「データ境界＝DB境界」として write-up に書く
   478	- 秘書の sweep（A段エンドポイント／B段 Runtime 経由どちらも）は台帳の全テナントを順に処理し、テナント別件数を返す。Scheduler・Runtime は無変更
   479	- シード投入は `--database` で対象DBを選ぶ（既存引数）。検証用に第2DB `kd-tenant-b` を `gcloud firestore databases create --database=kd-tenant-b --location=asia-northeast1 --type=firestore-native` で作り、小さなシードを入れて分離を実機確認する（後始末: DB削除）
   480	- Firestore Security Rules はDBごと。クライアントSDK直読み拒否の方針（§3）は各DBで同じ
   481	
   482	### 16.3 データ源の差し替え点（FR28〜30）
   483	
   484	```
   485	SourceConnector.sync(store, owner_employee_id, today) -> SyncSummary{tasks, schedules, mails}   # 冪等
   486	  ├─ SeedConnector            : 無操作（シード投入済み）
   487	  └─ GoogleWorkspaceConnector : Tasks / Calendar / Gmail の REST を google-auth の AuthorizedSession で呼ぶ（読み取り専用スコープ）。新規依存なし
   488	```
   489	
   490	- **sync-then-detect**: `SecretaryService.run_sweep` の先頭で、テナントの全所有者（agents 登録済み＋identities 登録済み）に対して `connector.sync` を実行してから検知に入る。`seed` では無操作なので既存の挙動・テストは不変。`SOURCE_CONNECTOR=seed|google_workspace`（既定 seed）
   491	- **所有者↔Googleアカウント**: 原則 `identities`（email）から。作者本人のADC（ユーザー資格情報）は自分のデータしか読めないため、env `GWS_SELF_EMPLOYEE_ID`（例 `emp_jordan_lee`）で「ADCの持ち主＝この社員」と宣言する単独モードを持つ。複数社員を読む本番形（サービスアカウント＋ドメイン全体委任）は手順のみREADMEに書き、実動確認はしない
   492	- **Tasks → tasks**: 全タスクリストの未完了タスク（title / notes→description / due / updated / status）。`task_id = gws_task_<tasklist>_<id>`。**リスケ計数**: 既存レコードの due と異なれば `reschedule_count += 1`（秘書が覚える）。`created_at` は初回同期日時、`status_changed_at` は status 変化を検出した同期日時（取得不能のため着手なしシグナルは実質0。§14.3は不変）。Tasks側で完了→`done`
   493	- **Calendar → schedules**: 今日〜N日先（既定3）の予定→ `meeting_prep`（due=開始日）、昨日〜今日の終了済み予定→ `meeting_review`。`item_id = gws_cal_<eventId>_<kind>`。経費・週報・ジャーナルの期日はカレンダーからは作らない（シード/社内ルール由来のまま）
   494	- **Gmail → mail_seeds**: 直近N日（既定7）・上限M件（既定20）の受信メール。本文は text/plain 優先でK文字（既定2000）に切り詰め。`mail_id = gws_mail_<msgId>`、既存IDは再投入しない、processed=false。**件名・本文はログ・監査payloadに出さない**。`GWS_GMAIL_ENABLED`（既定 false）で個別に有効化（作者判断で省略可）
   495	- 同期の失敗（API拒否・スコープ不足）は sweep の結果に `sync_errors` として件数で返し、検知は続行する（部分失敗で朝のカードを失わない）。認証情報・メール内容はエラーメッセージに含めない
   496	- 実動確認: `scripts/gws_probe.py`（件数と種別だけを表示。内容を出さない）→ ローカル InMemory 起動＋`SOURCE_CONNECTOR=google_workspace GWS_SELF_EMPLOYEE_ID=emp_jordan_lee` で sweep→digest に実データ由来のリマインド／停滞カードが現れることを確認。**収録はシードで行う**
   497	
   498	### 16.4 変更範囲
   499	
   500	- 触る: `server.py`（依存の置換・`/api/me`・require_self・ContextRouter）、`secretary.py`（sweep先頭の sync 呼び出し・テナントループは呼び出し側）、新規 `auth.py` / `tenancy.py` / `connectors/{base,seed,google_workspace}.py`、`store.py`/`firestore_store.py`（identities コレクションの追加のみ）、seeds（identities）、UI（`/api/me` を見てペルソナ切替を隠す）、README
   501	- 触らない: matching / transmission / schemas（マスク）/ 配送・同意・監査のロジック、B段 `secretary_agent`、Scheduler
   502	- テスト: 既存97＋13は demo_key/seed で不変。追加: 認証（検証成功・失敗・403・demo素通し）、テナント分離（2テナントInMemory横断ゼロ）、コネクタ写像（フェイクHTTP）、リスケ計数、sweepのsync-then-detect、`/api/me`
   503	
   504	### 16.5 撤退線・未決
   505	
   506	- 8/27 朝までに反証を通らない部品は「差し替え点＋`Seed`/`DemoKey` 実装のみ残し、実アダプタは将来項目」に落とす（ユーザー合意）
   507	- IAPの実有効化の実機確認（別サービス）と Gmail の実動確認は spec 未決（任意）
     1	"""HTTP client for the Cloud Run knowledge-discovery secretary API.
     2	
     3	This package (src/secretary_agent) is deliberately independent of
     4	src/knowledge_discovery: it only talks to the already-deployed Cloud Run
     5	service over its public HTTP API (X-API-Key auth), exactly like any other
     6	GEAP-registered client would (design.md §14.7 "パッケージング").
     7	"""
     8	
     9	from __future__ import annotations
    10	
    11	import os
    12	from typing import Any
    13	
    14	import requests
    15	
    16	
    17	class SecretaryApiError(RuntimeError):
    18	    """Raised when the Cloud Run secretary API returns a non-2xx response or
    19	    is unreachable.
    20	
    21	    Callers (app.py's run_daily_sweep) must let this propagate rather than
    22	    swallow it: design §14.7 入口1 requires Cloud Run failures to make the
    23	    Agent Engine `:query` response itself non-2xx, so Cloud Scheduler can
    24	    detect and retry the failed sweep (C-31: failures are not silenced).
    25	    """
    26	
    27	
    28	class SecretaryApiClient:
    29	    """Thin client for POST /api/secretary/sweep and GET /api/secretary/digest."""
    30	
    31	    def __init__(
    32	        self,
    33	        base_url: str | None = None,
    34	        api_key: str | None = None,
    35	        session: requests.Session | None = None,
    36	        timeout: float | None = None,
    37	    ) -> None:
    38	        self._base_url = (base_url or os.environ["KD_API_BASE_URL"]).rstrip("/")
    39	        self._api_key = api_key or os.environ["KD_API_KEY"]
    40	        # Injectable so tests can substitute a fake Session instead of making
    41	        # real network calls.
    42	        self._session = session or requests.Session()
    43	        # B-1 (round-11): the first sweep after a reseed took ~31s in production
    44	        # (embedding_public + LLM question draft), so 30s produced a false
    45	        # failure. Default 120s stays inside the Scheduler attempt deadline (180s).
    46	        self._timeout = timeout if timeout is not None else float(os.environ.get("KD_API_TIMEOUT", "120"))
    47	
    48	    def _headers(self) -> dict[str, str]:
    49	        return {"X-API-Key": self._api_key}
    50	
    51	    def run_sweep(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
    52	        """POST /api/secretary/sweep (design §14.1, §14.7 入口1).
    53	
    54	        Raises SecretaryApiError on a non-2xx response or connection
    55	        failure.
    56	        """
    57	        try:
    58	            resp = self._session.post(
    59	                f"{self._base_url}/api/secretary/sweep",
    60	                headers=self._headers(),
    61	                timeout=self._timeout,
    62	            )
    63	        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as exc:
    64	            raise SecretaryApiError(f"sweep request failed: {exc}") from exc
    65	        if not (200 <= resp.status_code < 300):
    66	            raise SecretaryApiError(
    67	                f"sweep returned HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}"
    68	            )
    69	        return resp.json()
    70	
    71	    def fetch_digest(self, employee_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    72	        """GET /api/secretary/digest?employee_id=<employee_id> (design §14.2, §14.8).
    73	
    74	        Raises SecretaryApiError on a non-2xx response or connection
    75	        failure.
    76	        """
    77	        try:
    78	            resp = self._session.get(
    79	                f"{self._base_url}/api/secretary/digest",
    80	                params={"employee_id": employee_id},
    81	                headers=self._headers(),
    82	                timeout=self._timeout,
    83	            )
    84	        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as exc:
    85	            raise SecretaryApiError(f"digest request failed: {exc}") from exc
    86	        if not (200 <= resp.status_code < 300):
    87	            raise SecretaryApiError(
    88	                f"digest returned HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}"
    89	            )
    90	        return resp.json()
    61	def _build_client() -> SecretaryApiClient:
    62	    """Factory seam so tests can substitute a fake client without touching
    63	    real env vars or the network."""
    64	    return SecretaryApiClient()
    65	
    66	
    67	def get_my_digest(tool_context: ToolContext) -> dict:
    68	    """Fetch today's morning digest (reminders + open cards) for the current
    69	    session's user.
    70	
    71	    Takes no employee_id argument by design (§14.7 C-34): the owner is
    72	    always the session's own user_id, so this tool can never be used to
    73	    read someone else's digest.
    74	    """
    75	    return _build_client().fetch_digest(employee_id=tool_context.user_id)
    76	
    77	
    78	def build_secretary_llm_agent() -> LlmAgent:
    79	    """Builds the read-only dialogue agent (入口2)."""
    80	    return LlmAgent(
    81	        name="secretary",
    82	        model=_GlobalGemini(model=_MODEL_NAME),
    83	        instruction=INSTRUCTION,
    84	        tools=[get_my_digest],
    85	    )
   235	    if not mail.body.strip() and not mail.subject.strip():
   236	        return None
   237	
   238	    if llm_client is not None:
   239	        try:
   240	            model_name = os.environ.get("GEMINI_MODEL", "gemini-3.7-flash")
   241	            existing_text = current_profile.get_full_text() if current_profile else ""
   242	            prompt = f"""You are analyzing a work email to see if it reveals tacit skills, current projects, or specialized background to add to the employee's professional profile.
   243	
   244	Current Profile:
   245	{existing_text}
   246	
   247	Email Subject: {mail.subject}
   248	Email Body: {mail.body}
   249	
   250	If this email contains meaningful professional experience or project context not yet in the profile, extract a suggested profile item in JSON format:
   251	{{
   252	  "item_key": "current_work" | "expertise" | "background",
   253	  "body_draft": "1-2 sentence description in English"
   254	}}
   255	If no meaningful knowledge is found, return null."""
   256	            response = llm_client.models.generate_content(
   257	                model=model_name,
   258	                contents=prompt,
   259	            )
   260	            resp_text = response.text.strip() if hasattr(response, "text") and response.text else ""
   261	            cleaned = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*", "", resp_text)
   262	            cleaned = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", cleaned).strip()
   263	            if cleaned == "null":
   264	                # Explicit "no diff": LLM was reachable and deliberately found
   265	                # nothing worth proposing. Do NOT fall through to the heuristic
   266	                # (design §14.5-1: null must be expressible; V-8/S-6).
   267	                return None
   268	            if not cleaned:
   269	                # Empty response is an API failure, not a deliberate null.
   270	                # Leave the mail unconsumed so the next sweep retries (R-2).
   271	                return EXTRACTION_FAILED
   272	            data = json.loads(cleaned)
   273	            if isinstance(data, dict) and "body_draft" in data:
   274	                key = data.get("item_key", "current_work")
   275	                body = data.get("body_draft", "").strip()
   276	                if body:
   277	                    return key, body
   278	            return None
   279	        except Exception:
   280	            # LLM call/parse failed. Do NOT copy mail text as a fallback (that
   281	            # re-opens S-6) and do NOT consume the mail — retry next sweep (R-2).
   282	            return EXTRACTION_FAILED
   283	
   284	    # Heuristic extraction fallback (reached only when llm_client is None,
   285	    # e.g. a local demo without GEMINI_API_KEY — never the default path, V-8/S-6).
   286	    subj = mail.subject.strip()
   287	    body = mail.body.strip()
   288	    if len(body) > 10:
   289	        return "current_work", f"{subj}: {body}"
   290	    return None

design/knowledge-discovery/spec.md:11:2. **テナント＝Firestoreデータベース分離**: 全コレクションに tenant_id 列を足す方式は触る範囲が広くフィルタ漏れが即データ混在になるため採らない。Firestore のマルチデータベースを使い **テナント＝データベース** とする（`FirestoreStore` は既に database 引数を持つ）。テナント台帳（tenant_id → database / 許可メールドメイン）を1つ持ち、認証で確定したテナントのDBだけを開く。「データ境界＝プロセス境界」の設計哲学と同型。デモは1テナント（`(default)` DB）
design/knowledge-discovery/spec.md:19:1. **秘書エージェントの追加（M3）**: タスクの停滞を検知し、プレビュー検索（配送なし）で候補まで用意した上で、本人の依頼確定があって初めて既存のつながりレーンに合流する。「気づく・調べる」までは自律、「人を巻き込む」は本人確定必須。停滞検知は説明可能性のためルールベース（重み付きスコア＋2段閾値）とし、LLM判定を用いない。検知事実は本人以外に通知しない（監視ツール化の防止。Starmind等への対抗軸「監視型の対極」を維持）。**秘書自体は審査上の主張点にしない**——大元機能の説得性を支える土台と位置づける。
design/knowledge-discovery/spec.md:23:5. **GEAP採用範囲の確定**: Agent Registry を実採用し、秘書エージェントは2段構えで Agent Runtime へ載せる。Gemini Enterprise（SaaS）のスケジューラ利用と Gemini Spark の実接続は却下（検討済み・却下を参照）。
design/knowledge-discovery/spec.md:91:14. **テナント＝DB分離（v8）**: テナント台帳（tenant_id → Firestore database / 許可メールドメイン）。認証で確定したテナントのDBのみを開く。秘書の巡回はテナント台帳を回す。デモは1テナント
